- **CONVENTIONS**: **`${ARGS}` (skill-constructed)** is used by *thin wrappers* whose calling skill assembles a typed-flag argument list. The skill conducts the interview, resolves defaults, applies vault context, and then invokes the slash command with a fully-formed flag set. Because the wrapper body cannot contain conditionals, all orchestration lives in the skill — the wrapper just shells out. ([linxule/carrel](https://github.com/linxule/carrel/blob/HEAD/commands/CONVENTIONS.md) · ★2)
